# Current Apply Order — v8 / v9 / v10

1. Base bundle: `wowas_final_edition_BRAXON_ready_v8.zip`
2. Polish bundle: `wowas_v9_polish_patch.tar.gz`
3. Update 10 bundle: this directory / tarball

## Runtime rule
Braxon should treat later-numbered bundles as newer authority. Do not overwrite the base files destructively unless you explicitly decide to collapse the layers into a merged monolith later.

## v10 prose / tone authority patch
- Apply `patches/PROSE_AND_TONE_GUIDE.json` as the active manuscript-generation prose law.
- Register supporting note: `patches/v10/wowas_prose_and_tone_patch_v10.md`
- This guide governs POV, Pip cognition/perception, dialogue/play insertion, chapter expansion, relationship truth distinctions, and anti-catchphrase safeguards.
- When older notes conflict at the prose/voice/manuscript layer, prefer this guide unless a later addendum explicitly overrides it.

## v10 dialogue / play insertion authority patch
- Apply `patches/v10/wowas_dialogue_and_play_insertion_patch_v10.md` during canon-to-manuscript expansion.
- Treat dossier notes, side notes, relationship notes, and support-cast notes as potential manuscript-bearing dialogue and play scenes.
- Use with `patches/PROSE_AND_TONE_GUIDE.json`.
- Prefer lived dialogue, play, comfort, curiosity, and aftermath over connective summary whenever chapter authority permits.

## v10 book dialogue seed registry patch
- Apply `patches/v10/wowas_book_dialogue_seed_registry_v10.md` during canon-to-manuscript expansion.
- Use this registry as the first lookup surface when a beat resolves only into scaffold language, repeated "tone and structure lock," or reconstruction filler.
- Prefer the strongest matching dialogue/play insertion target before adding connective summary.

## v10 anti-placeholder expansion patch
- Apply `patches/v10/wowas_anti_placeholder_expansion_patch_v10.md` during canon-to-manuscript expansion.
- Treat scaffold language such as "tone and structure lock," "the book finds its law," and repeated reconstruction filler as expansion triggers, not final manuscript content.
- Resolve such placeholders through lived scene realization before allowing connective summary.

## v10 books 24-25 priority override patch
- Apply `patches/v10/wowas_books_24_25_priority_override_patch_v10.md` during canon-to-manuscript expansion.
- For `Born To Die` and `Death Is Rebirth`, prefer accountability, mortal-wound, grief, waking, changed-return, obligation, and fragile joy scenes before connective summary.
- Treat these two books as highest-priority manuscript debt whenever scaffold language remains.

## v10 scene authority cleanup patch
- Apply `patches/v10/wowas_scene_authority_cleanup_patch_v10.md` during canon-to-manuscript expansion.
- Distinguish true scene anchors from editorial shorthand, pressure-pattern prompts, and reconstruction filler.
- Do not let scene-index density or repeated reconstruction rows count as manuscript completion.
- Prefer concrete placed/source scenes over rewritten shorthand and reconstruction padding.

## v10 book build selection order patch
- Apply `patches/v10/wowas_book_build_selection_order_patch_v10.md` during canon-to-manuscript expansion.
- Select rows by strongest scene-family leader rather than densest row cluster.
- Cluster repeated pressure-pattern, beat-end, equation-title, and reconstruction families before build selection.
- Keep concrete anchors dominant and cap scaffold-heavy families so they cannot flood manuscript generation.
